Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test" (a qualifying-relative rule; the project refuses dependent.multiple_support only on its qualifying-relative test). executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 8,616 of 9,011 agree; 164 project-unknown, 140 project-refuses, 66 candidate-unknown, 16 value, 9 candidate-refuses; on completed returns: 21 candidate-unknown, 7 value, 2 candidate-refuses. The reply's household test is relationship household_member alone, or qr_all_year and lawful: a household member who did not live with you all year passes. Every completed-return value (7, project no and candidate yes) is a household_member entry with dependent.qr_all_year no (classm.f8962.3 and its .deepseek and .gpt variants, classm.tcw.5, generated 135, 269 and 497). The reply also refuses dependent.multiple_support on the whole line, so it refuses or asks the agreement for qualifying children (21 completed-return differences), and its h_can_claim lets a joint return pass when you can be claimed as a dependent (classm.sc.2, a refused return). The project's refusals (claimed_no_tin 105, qc_tiebreak 26, income_exception, qr_kidnapped, kidnap_residency) are cited screens. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Open: the interface doesn't say whether dc_count is filtered. Chosen reading: it is 1 for a qualifying person and 0 otherwise. A choose can't return a Count, so this uses percent_floor of 1 cent over 100 cents, which gives 1, or 0 when the flag is 0.

The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return". executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 4,657 of 9,011 agree; 4,156 candidate-unknown, 190 candidate-refuses, 5 project-refuses, 2 candidate-shows-skipped, 1 value; on completed returns: 1,294 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. The reply's American opportunity test requires dependent.edu_tin_issued (an issued number) where the project, under the p970 p. 11 rule, accepts a timely ITIN or ATIN application (dependent.tin) and refuses only the lifetime learning credit for such a student (dependent.edu_llc_tin_refused): f1.t113.dep_aoc_applied, line 27 $4,000.00 against $0.00 (incomplete). It also declares dependent.edu_over as a raw refusal on every entry, so the 1,290 completed differences ask it of non-students. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return". executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 4,657 of 9,011 agree; 4,156 candidate-unknown, 190 candidate-refuses, 5 project-refuses, 2 candidate-shows-skipped, 1 value; on completed returns: 1,294 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. The reply's American opportunity test requires dependent.edu_tin_issued (an issued number) where the project, under the p970 p. 11 rule, accepts a timely ITIN or ATIN application (dependent.tin) and refuses only the lifetime learning credit for such a student (dependent.edu_llc_tin_refused): f1.t113.dep_aoc_applied, line 27 $4,000.00 against $0.00 (incomplete). It also declares dependent.edu_over as a raw refusal on every entry, so the 1,290 completed differences ask it of non-students. Project stands.

The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return". executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 4,657 of 9,011 agree; 4,156 candidate-unknown, 190 candidate-refuses, 5 project-refuses, 2 candidate-shows-skipped, 1 value; on completed returns: 1,294 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. The reply's American opportunity test requires dependent.edu_tin_issued (an issued number) where the project, under the p970 p. 11 rule, accepts a timely ITIN or ATIN application (dependent.tin) and refuses only the lifetime learning credit for such a student (dependent.edu_llc_tin_refused): f1.t113.dep_aoc_applied, line 27 $4,000.00 against $0.00 (incomplete). It also declares dependent.edu_over as a raw refusal on every entry, so the 1,290 completed differences ask it of non-students. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: aqee.ws4_llc is read as worksheet line 4 for the lifetime learning credit: the expenses required to be paid to the institution, minus the adjustments, with a floor of zero.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11, "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; i8863 p. 9, Line 31. executable-triage-v2 (rerun of triage.py's triage() with the witness cap lifted, record in scratch `/tmp/adj-bklg-dep-f2441/triage/dependent.json`; 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,946 partial records from 40 bases; 9,011 comparisons): 4,652 of 9,011 agree; 4,162 candidate-unknown, 189 candidate-refuses, 6 project-refuses, 2 value; on completed returns: 1,294 candidate-unknown, 2 candidate-refuses. As line 27 (edu_over asked of every entry: 1,290 completed differences); the 2 values are on refused returns (generated 47 and 470, dependent.tin no). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person, counted once)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent (flag amount used to count a qualifying person)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: dependent.h_qc_rel
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_test
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 at the end of 2025 and younger than you (or your spouse if filing jointly), or under age 24, a full-time student and younger than you (or your spouse), or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_lived_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025 (see Exception to time lived with you, and Kidnapped child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the kidnapped-child rule (Pub. 501) has conditions about time lived with you before the kidnapping and after the return, and those facts aren't collected. Chosen reading: a child answered as kidnapped is treated as meeting the residency test. Births, deaths and placements are already reflected in the months input."

  - id: dependent.h_qc_nojr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Meets the qualifying child relationship, age, residency and support tests (before the joint return test)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child (Step 1): also isn't filing a joint return for 2025 (or files only to claim a refund)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_nojr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
    note: "Open: when the child is also the qualifying child of another person, the tiebreaker rules apply, and the facts they need (parentage, AGI, the other person's claim) aren't collected. Chosen reading: the filer is treated as the person who can claim the child."

  - id: dependent.h_qr_direct
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relative who doesn't have to live with you (child, sibling, parent or ancestor, aunt or uncle, step-parent or in-law)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_spouse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint return: the person is related to your spouse"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.spouse_relative, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_household
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_member
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Member of household or relationship test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_direct, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_spouse, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_household, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_not_qc_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Wasn't a qualifying child of any taxpayer for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr_nogi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative tests other than gross income (relationship or household, not a qualifying child, support)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_member, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_not_qc_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative (Step 4): also had gross income of less than $5,200 in 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_nogi, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}

  - id: dependent.h_citizen_test
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_can_claim
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing a joint return, or you couldn't be claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent (Steps 1, 2 and 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_can_claim, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support]

  - id: dependent.h_under17
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Was the child under age 17 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: you can claim the child tax credit for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_under17, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.ctc_ssn is read as the Step 3 question 4 requirement that you (or your spouse if filing jointly) have an SSN valid for employment issued before the due date, with the other joint spouse holding an SSN or ITIN."

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 5: you can claim the credit for other dependents for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.all_tin is read as the Step 5 question 1 requirement that you and your spouse (if filing jointly) have an SSN or ITIN issued, or applied for, on or before the due date."

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 per other dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 per qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.h_household_only
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Person who is your dependent only because the person lived with you for all of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_direct, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_spouse, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.h_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 when the care was provided (a child who turned 13 during the year qualifies for the part of the year before)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying person: a qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent, or a person not able to care for themselves who lived with you for more than half the year and is (or would be, except for gross income, a joint return, or your being claimable) your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_under13, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_test, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_nojr, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_nogi, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    note: "Age at the end of 2025 of 12 or less means the person was never over 12 in 2025. Age 14 or more means they were over 12 all year. At age 13, the dc_over12_care answer decides."

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments: tax-free educational assistance received in 2025 and in 2026, and refunds (Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 3)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Education credit can be figured for this claimed dependent (expenses paid, Form 1098-T requirement met, student TIN issued by the due date)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 permit the American opportunity credit for this student, and the institution's EIN is provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Go to line 31 for this student (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.edu_over]
    note: "aqee.ws4_llc is read as worksheet line 4 for the lifetime learning credit: the expenses required to be paid to the institution, minus the adjustments, with a floor of zero."

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 5 (number of qualifying children x $2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 7 (number of other dependents x $500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's share of Schedule 8812 line 16b (qualifying children x $1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_per}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2: this person lived with you for more than half of the year and is your dependent (not only because the person lived with you all year), or is your unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_household_only, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.multiple_support]
    note: "The exclusions for children of divorced or separated parents, multiple support agreements and married qualifying children are handled by the screening refusals."

  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2 is met for this person only by counting an absence from which return wasn't reasonably expected, or during which you didn't keep up the home"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.absence_no_return, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC (relationship, age, not filing a joint return, lived with you in the United States for more than half of 2025)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_rel, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.married]
    note: "Open: the tiebreaker for a child of more than one person can't be resolved from the inputs. Chosen reading: the filer is treated as able to claim the child."

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child for the EIC who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_dc_flag
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if this person is a qualifying person for Form 2441, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_one_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons this entry contributes (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_flag, whole: {const: 100}, cap: 1}
    note: "Open: the interface doesn't say whether dc_count is filtered. Chosen reading: it is 1 for a qualifying person and 0 otherwise. A choose can't return a Count, so this uses percent_floor of 1 cent over 100 cents, which gives 1, or 0 when the flag is 0."

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet 1-2: modified AGI of a dependent who is required to file an income tax return because their income meets the filing threshold"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
```
```
